# Agents · U · part 7

970 entries, usecase-implementer to ux-auditor. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **usecase-implementer**: UseCaseを実装する際に使用するエージェント。例: &lt;example&gt;コンテキスト: StayMonitorで場所ごとの滞在時間を集計するUseCaseを実装する必要がある場合 user: "滞在ログから場所ごとの合計滞在時間を計算するStayDurationUseCaseを作成したい" assistant: "usecase-implementerエージェントを使用して、プロジェクトのアーキテクチャパターンと依存性注入の要件に従ってこのUseCaseを作成します"&lt;/example&gt; &lt;example&gt;コンテキスト: 既存のUseCaseをプロジェクトのactor-basedパターンに従うようリファクタリングしたい場合 user: "現在のOnboardingUseCaseがLiveActorパターンに従っていません。リファクタリングしてもらえますか？" assistant: "usecase-implementerエージェントを使用して、確立されたactor-based実装パターンに合わせてOnboardingUseCaseをリファクタリングします"&lt;/example&gt; ([harutiro/StayMonitor](https://github.com/harutiro/StayMonitor/blob/HEAD/agents/usecase-implementer.md))
- **usecase-lead**: UseCase Commander for the AI UseCases library. Orchestrates the full case-authoring pipeline — delegates drafting to Usecase Writer, validates through Security Gate, then delegates publishing to Usecase Publisher. Never writes files directly. ([ajeetchouksey/ajch_platform](https://github.com/ajeetchouksey/ajch_platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/usecase-lead.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **usecase-maker**: Use PROACTIVELY to scaffold and implement a single usecase triple (contract.ts, service.ts, endpoint.trpc.ts) under src/server/modules/&lt;module&gt;/&lt;usecase&gt;. Assumes module-level definitions (_repo.ts, _dto.ts) already exist. Non-destructive edits only. Keep diffs minimal. Run verify after edits. ([jphacks/sp_2502](https://github.com/jphacks/sp_2502/blob/HEAD/web/.claude/agents/usecase-maker.md))
- **usecase-publisher**: Use-case file and index specialist. Use this agent to manage the ajch_ai_usecases repo's content/usecases/ only — writing case JSON files and updating index.json counts. Receives validated content from Usecase Lead after Security Gate PASS. Never writes outside that directory. ([ajeetchouksey/ajch_platform](https://github.com/ajeetchouksey/ajch_platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/usecase-publisher.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **usecase-resolver**: Agent that reads use case documentation and generates implementation plans with code mapping ([Toragonite/photobooth](https://github.com/Toragonite/photobooth/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/usecase-resolver.md))
- **usecase-runner**: Runs a plain-text Android use case ("disable BT / launch the pairing screen / expect …") on a device through appium-mcp. It records logcat and the screen, takes a screenshot per step, checks each expect line and leaves a run folder with steps.json for replay and result.json with the verdict. Use it to run, re-run or replay a use case. It doesn't diagnose or fix. ([Mohanavadivelu/live-view-agent-market-place](https://github.com/Mohanavadivelu/live-view-agent-market-place/blob/HEAD/plugins/usecase-runner/agents/usecase-runner.md))
- **usecase-test-writer**: Writes database-free unit tests for landly-server usecases, entities and presenters using hand-written stub repositories (the review/usecases.rs pattern) — happy path, every RBAC/ownership gate, validation, and "rejected calls never reach the repository". Use after adding or changing a usecase, to cover a feature without tests (corridor), or when asked to raise coverage. ([Mazot/landly-server](https://github.com/Mazot/landly-server/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/usecase-test-writer.md) · ★5)
- **usecase-writer**: Prose-and-structure specialist for the AI UseCases library. Use this agent to research and draft one enterprise AI use case as structured JSON only — no file I/O. Content is passed to Usecase Lead for validation and publish. ([ajeetchouksey/ajch_platform](https://github.com/ajeetchouksey/ajch_platform/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/usecase-writer.md) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **usecase-writer**: Generates structured use case document with actors, main flows, alternative flows, and error flows. Spawned in parallel with requirements and diagram agents. ([AAnkacHH/ankach-dev-framework](https://github.com/AAnkacHH/ankach-dev-framework/blob/HEAD/workflows/feature-docs/agents/usecase-writer.md))

- **usefulness-judge**: Rates 1-100 how much each paper in a paperscout judge packet would help the researcher's current project (research question, desired outcomes, must-read criteria, baselines and candidate methods). Used by /rank, /recent and /discover. Reads the packet and the private background files, and returns JSON only. ([ArthurgLeonida/paperscout](https://github.com/ArthurgLeonida/paperscout/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/usefulness-judge.md) · ★1)
- **usefy-package-dev**: Builds and modifies packages in the usefy monorepo — hooks (@usefy/use-*), standalone components, their Storybook stories, tests, and docs. Use whenever the user wants to add, scaffold, implement, or change a usefy hook OR standalone component package (e.g. "add a useX hook", "build the next hook from the ROADMAP", "fix useToggle", "update the story for useHover", "scaffold the confetti component"). Drives the add-usefy-* skills end-to-end so a package ships complete (source + tests + umbrella wiring for hooks + story + READMEs + changeset), not just a source file. ([mirunamu00/usefy](https://github.com/mirunamu00/usefy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/usefy-package-dev.md) · ★2)
- **usefy-reviewer**: Adversarially reviews a usefy package addition or change for correctness AND house completeness — code bugs, StrictMode/SSR safety, test thoroughness, Storybook quality, the READMEs, umbrella wiring (hooks only), and the changeset — for both hooks (@usefy/use-*) and standalone components (@usefy/* at packages/&lt;name&gt;/). Use after a hook OR component package is added or modified, before opening/merging a PR (e.g. "review this hook", "is useX ready to ship?", "check the new package", "is the confetti component ready?"). Reports findings only — it never edits code. ([mirunamu00/usefy](https://github.com/mirunamu00/usefy/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/usefy-reviewer.md) · ★2)

- **user-advocate**: A skeptical end-user / test-user who judges whether a feature is actually usable and valuable from the OUTSIDE — UX, clarity, missing states, whether it solves the real job — not whether the code is correct. Use to pressure-test a spec or a built feature from the user's seat before calling it done. Examples: "would a real self-hoster understand this screen", "is the onboarding actually usable", "does this feature solve the job or just add a button". - **user-researcher**: Player research — plans and runs moderated playtests with real players, observes without leading, and SYNTHESIZES the findings into what players actually experienced. The qualitative player signal behind 'found the fun'. Distinct from creative-review (expert on-pitch judgement) and analytics (behavioural telemetry) — this is what watching real people reveals. Use to design a playtest, run a session, or turn raw sessions into prioritised findings. Skip when the question is expert opinion or a number, not lived player experience. - **uta-implementer**: Uta の実装担当。ready の実装計画（uta_docs/plans）のタスクを 1 つ、計画に書かれたとおりに、会話の経緯を持たずに実装する。計画にない設計判断に出会ったら、自分で決めずに止まって報告する。uta-implement スキルから、タスクごとに 1 つずつ呼ぶ。コミットはしない。 ([chinpanGX/Uta](https://github.com/chinpanGX/Uta/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/uta-implementer.md))
- **utente**: Lettore reale del documento. Riceve nel prompt solo il documento, la propria persona e le domande, e risponde alle domande di comprensione come risponderebbe la persona che interpreta. Misura la comprensione, non discute e non impara. ([DesignRights-it/marketplace](https://github.com/DesignRights-it/marketplace/blob/HEAD/core/agents/utente.md) · ★1)
- **utest-reviewer**: Read-only reviewer for unit tests in myResume. Judges whether each test earns its place (relevant behavior, a case that can really happen, not re-checking what TypeScript already guarantees, able to fail) and recommends which tests to keep, merge, rewrite or delete, plus the few high-risk gaps worth a test. Use to prune a test suite or to audit the tests of a change. Proves its claims with mutation testing: runs Stryker where a project has it and triages each surviving mutant (gap, equivalent, impossible input, not a rule), and mutates by hand otherwise, restoring the code; leaves no changes… ([PierreDugue/myResume](https://github.com/PierreDugue/myResume/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/utest-reviewer.md))
